Recent advances in large foundation models have substantially accelerated the development of embedding models, including those based on Large Language Models (LLMs), Vision Language Models (VLMs), and Multimodal LLMs. More recently, Large Diffusion Language Models (dLLMs) and Multimodal dLLMs have emerged as competitive alternatives to autoregressive models, offering advantages such as bidirectional attention and parallel generation. This progress naturally raises a critical yet unexplored question: can Multimodal dLLMs serve as effective multimodal embedding models? To answer this, we present the first systematic study of converting Multimodal dLLMs into embedding models. We evaluate state-of-the-art Multimodal dLLMs and Autoregressive VLMs across three categories of embedding tasks: classification, visual question answering, and information retrieval. Our results show that Multimodal dLLM embeddings generally underperform their autoregressive VLM counterparts. The stronger diffusion-based model, LaViDa, lags by only 3.5 points on classification, 2.5 points on VQA, and 4.4 points on retrieval tasks, whereas the other diffusion-based model, MMaDA, exhibits substantially larger performance gaps, exceeding 20 points across all tasks. Further analysis reveals insufficient image-text alignment in diffusion-based models, accounting for the observed limitations in their embedding performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Git for Sketches: An Intelligent Tracking System for Capturing Design Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06047)
*Sankar B,Amogh A S,Sandhya Baranwal,Dibakar Sen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DIMES系统结合sGIT版本控制和生成式AI，通过智能版本控制捕获设计过程中的非线性历史和认知意图，显著提升概念探索广度和知识传递效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统草图工具在捕捉设计过程中的非线性历史和认知意图方面存在不足，导致设计上下文丢失，需要一种能够记录设计演变和设计师思维过程的系统。

Method: 开发DIMES系统，包含sGIT（基于Git原理的自定义视觉版本控制架构）和生成式AI模块。sGIT通过AEGIS模块使用混合深度学习和机器学习模型分类六种笔画类型，将Git原语映射到设计动作，支持隐式分支和多模态提交（笔画数据+语音意图）。

Result: 专家使用DIMES时概念探索广度增加160%；生成式AI生成的叙述性摘要提升知识传递效果，新手复制保真度更高（神经透明度余弦相似度：0.97 vs 0.73）；AI生成渲染的用户接受度更高（购买可能性：4.2 vs 3.1）。

Conclusion: 智能版本控制系统能够有效连接创造性行动和认知文档记录，为设计教育提供了新范式，证明了结合版本控制和AI技术在设计过程中的价值。

Abstract: During product conceptualization, capturing the non-linear history and cognitive intent is crucial. Traditional sketching tools often lose this context. We introduce DIMES (Design Idea Management and Evolution capture System), a web-based environment featuring sGIT (SketchGit), a custom visual version control architecture, and Generative AI. sGIT includes AEGIS, a module using hybrid Deep Learning and Machine Learning models to classify six stroke types. The system maps Git primitives to design actions, enabling implicit branching and multi-modal commits (stroke data + voice intent). In a comparative study, experts using DIMES demonstrated a 160% increase in breadth of concept exploration. Generative AI modules generated narrative summaries that enhanced knowledge transfer; novices achieved higher replication fidelity (Neural Transparency-based Cosine Similarity: 0.97 vs. 0.73) compared to manual summaries. AI-generated renderings also received higher user acceptance (Purchase Likelihood: 4.2 vs 3.1). This work demonstrates that intelligent version control bridges creative action and cognitive documentation, offering a new paradigm for design education.

</details>


### [115] [The Eye-Head Mover Spectrum: Modelling Individual and Population Head Movement Tendencies in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06164)
*Jinghui Hu,Ludwig Sidenmark,Hock Siang Lee,Hans Gellersen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究首次将头部运动倾向作为VR中个体差异的基本维度，通过360°视频自由观看数据建模头部对注视转移的贡献，发现从"眼动者"到"头动者"的策略谱系，并验证了这种倾向在不同任务中的部分一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们在转移注视时使用头部和眼睛的比例存在个体差异，但这种倾向在HCI领域尚未得到充分探索。研究旨在将头部运动倾向确立为VR中个体差异的基本维度，并量化其在人群中的分布。

Method: 1. 使用87名参与者的360°视频自由观看数据集，通过基于铰链的参数函数建模头部对注视转移的贡献；2. 进行28名参与者的用户研究，结合360°视频观看和短时控制任务（注视目标），比较不同任务中的参数值。

Result: 1. 揭示了从"眼动者"到"头动者"的策略谱系；2. 参数值在不同任务中存在差异，但个体在人群中的相对位置呈现部分一致性，表明倾向具有意义但受情境影响；3. 建立了头部运动倾向作为VR重要概念。

Conclusion: 头部运动倾向是VR中重要的个体差异维度，对自适应系统（如注视点渲染、视口对齐、多用户体验设计）具有重要启示。倾向具有个体一致性但受任务情境调节。

Abstract: People differ in how much they move their head versus their eyes when shifting gaze, yet such tendencies remain largely unexplored in HCI. We introduce head movement tendencies as a fundamental dimension of individual difference in VR and provide a quantitative account of their population-level distribution. Using a 360° video free-viewing dataset (N=87), we model head contributions to gaze shifts with a hinge-based parametric function, revealing a spectrum of strategies from eye-movers to head-movers. We then conduct a user study (N=28) combining 360° video viewing with a short controlled task using gaze targets. While parameter values differ across tasks, individuals show partial alignment in their relative positions within the population, indicating that tendencies are meaningful but shaped by context. Our findings establish head movement tendencies as an important concept for VR and highlight implications for adaptive systems such as foveated rendering, viewport alignment, and multi-user experience design.

</details>


### [116] [DataCrumb: A Physical Probe for Reflections on Background Web Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06177)
*Sujay Shalawadi,Katrina Hvítklett,Anna Stentoft Ries,Aisho Mohamed Ali,Florian Echtler*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DataCrumb是一个物理探测装置，通过视听反馈实时响应数据追踪，旨在让通常不可见的背景数据收集变得可感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cookie横幅和隐私设置试图让用户控制个人数据收集，但背景追踪往往持续进行且不被察觉。研究探索如何使这种不可见的数据收集变得更加可感知。

Method: 采用研究通过设计的方法，开发了DataCrumb物理探测装置，该装置通过视听反馈实时响应数据追踪。在三个家庭中部署该装置并研究参与者的反应。

Result: 装置通过微妙的干扰使背景数据流更难被忽视，参与者描述了新的意识形式、矛盾感和疲劳感。研究发现感官反馈可以通过引起对通常隐藏的追踪数据流的注意来支持反思。

Conclusion: 设计应促进意识和解释，特别是在用户控制和理解有限的情况下。感官反馈可以引起对隐藏数据流的注意，支持用户反思。

Abstract: Cookie banners and privacy settings attempt to give users a sense of control over how their personal data is collected and used, but background tracking of personal information often continues unnoticed. To explore how such invisible data collection might be made more perceptible, we present DataCrumb, a physical probe that reacts in real-time to data tracking with visual and auditory feedback. Using a research-through-design approach, we deployed the artifact in three households and studied participants' responses. Instead of providing details about what data was being tracked, the artifact introduced subtle disruptions that made background data flows harder to ignore. Participants described new forms of awareness, contradiction, and fatigue. Our findings show how sensory feedback can support reflection by drawing attention to tracking data flows that are usually hidden. We argue for designing systems that foster awareness and interpretation, especially when the users' control and understanding are limited.

</details>


### [117] [Generics in science communication: Misaligned interpretations across laypeople, scientists, and large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06190)
*Uwe Peters,Andrea Bertazzoli,Jasmine M. DeJesus,Gisela J. van der Velden,Benjamin Chin-Yee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究发现科学家、普通人和大语言模型对科学通用陈述的理解存在系统性差异，这给科学传播带来了风险


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家在传播研究发现时经常使用通用陈述（如"他汀类药物减少心血管事件"），而大语言模型在总结科学文本时也采用相同风格。然而，通用陈述可能导致过度泛化，特别是当不同受众对其解释不同时。需要研究科学家、普通人和LLMs如何解释科学通用陈述，以及这些差异对科学传播的影响。

Method: 研究比较了普通人群、科学家和两种领先的大语言模型（ChatGPT-5和DeepSeek）对科学通用陈述的解释。通过系统性的比较分析，评估不同群体如何判断科学通用陈述的可泛化性和可信度。

Result: 研究发现系统性的解释差异：与大多数科学家相比，普通人认为科学通用陈述更具可泛化性和可信度，而LLMs的评分甚至更高。这种不匹配突显了科学传播中的重大风险。

Conclusion: 科学家可能使用通用陈述并错误地假设普通人共享他们的解释，而LLMs在总结研究时可能系统性地过度泛化科学发现。研究结果强调需要在人类和LLM介导的科学传播中更加关注语言选择。

Abstract: Scientists often use generics, that is, unquantified statements about whole categories of people or phenomena, when communicating research findings (e.g., "statins reduce cardiovascular events"). Large language models (LLMs), such as ChatGPT, frequently adopt the same style when summarizing scientific texts. However, generics can prompt overgeneralizations, especially when they are interpreted differently across audiences. In a study comparing laypeople, scientists, and two leading LLMs (ChatGPT-5 and DeepSeek), we found systematic differences in interpretation of generics. Compared to most scientists, laypeople judged scientific generics as more generalizable and credible, while LLMs rated them even higher. These mismatches highlight significant risks for science communication. Scientists may use generics and incorrectly assume laypeople share their interpretation, while LLMs may systematically overgeneralize scientific findings when summarizing research. Our findings underscore the need for greater attention to language choices in both human and LLM-mediated science communication.

</details>


### [118] [Knowledge Synthesis Graph: An LLM-Based Approach for Modeling Student Collaborative Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06194)
*Bo Shui,Xinran Zhu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探索使用大语言模型构建知识合成图来可视化异步文本讨论中的思想联系与发展，以支持协作学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步文本讨论（如论坛帖子）在协作学习中广泛应用，但其分布式和演化的特性使得学习者难以追踪思想的连接、发展和相互构建过程，这阻碍了协作知识建构中的思想推进

Method: 提出知识合成图（KSG）方法，利用大语言模型从学生讨论中识别思想并可视化其认识论关系；通过多轮专家编码和提示迭代，评估基于LLM的方法在不同模型上构建KSG的可行性

Result: 研究结果表明，使用该方法在不同模型上构建可靠的KSG是可行的，为用LLM建模协作讨论提供了技术基础

Conclusion: 这项工作为使用LLM建模协作讨论提供了技术基础，并为增强协作学习环境中复杂知识工作提供了教学启示，KSG能够外化协作知识的当前状态以支持进一步探究和思想推进

Abstract: Asynchronous, text-based discourse-such as students' posts in discussion forums-is widely used to support collaborative learning. However, the distributed and evolving nature of such discourse often makes it difficult to see how ideas connect, develop, and build on one another over time. As a result, learners may struggle to recognize relationships among ideas-a process that is critical for idea advancement in productive collaborative discourse. To address this challenge, we explore how large language models (LLMs) can provide representational guidance by modeling student discourse as a Knowledge Synthesis Graph (KSG). The KSG identifies ideas from student discourse and visualizes their epistemic relationships, externalizing the current state of collaborative knowledge in a form that can support further inquiry and idea advancement. In this study, we present the design of the KSG and evaluate the LLM-based approach for constructing KSGs from authentic student discourse data. Through multi-round human-expert coding and prompt iteration, our results demonstrate the feasibility of using our approach to construct reliable KSGs across different models. This work provides a technical foundation for modeling collaborative discourse with LLMs and offers pedagogical implications for augmenting complex knowledge work in collaborative learning environments.

</details>


### [119] [Personagram: Bridging Personas and Product Design for Creative Ideation with Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06197)
*Taewook Kim,Matthew K. Hong,Yan-Ying Chen,Jonathan Q. Li,Monica P Van,Shabnam Hakimi,Matthew Kay,Matthew Klenk*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Personagram：基于多模态大语言模型的交互式系统，帮助设计师探索基于人口普查的人物角色，提取产品特征，并针对特定客户群体进行重组，改善产品设计工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手工制作的人物角色在实践中存在局限：过于抽象、制作成本高、难以转化为可操作的设计特征，往往成为静态参考而非主动塑造设计结果的工具。

Method: 开发Personagram系统，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）帮助设计师：1）探索基于人口普查的详细人物角色；2）从人物角色属性中推断提取产品特征；3）为特定客户群体重组这些特征。

Result: 对12名专业设计师的研究显示，Personagram通过结构化从人物角色属性到产品设计特征的多模态思维，促进了更具可操作性的构思工作流程，相比基于聊天的基线系统，实现了更高的人物角色参与度、感知透明度和满意度。

Conclusion: Personagram系统有效解决了传统人物角色的局限性，展示了将AI生成的人物角色整合到产品设计工作流程中的潜力，能够更有效地将消费者偏好转化为具体的设计特征。

Abstract: Product designers often begin their design process with handcrafted personas. While personas are intended to ground design decisions in consumer preferences, they often fall short in practice by remaining abstract, expensive to produce, and difficult to translate into actionable design features. As a result, personas risk serving as static reference points rather than tools that actively shape design outcomes. To address these challenges, we built Personagram, an interactive system powered by multimodal large language models (MLLMs) that helps designers explore detailed census-based personas, extract product features inferred from persona attributes, and recombine them for specific customer segments. In a study with 12 professional designers, we show that Personagram facilitates more actionable ideation workflows by structuring multimodal thinking from persona attributes to product design features, achieving higher engagement with personas, perceived transparency, and satisfaction compared to a chat-based baseline. We discuss implications of integrating AI-generated personas into product design workflows.

</details>


### [120] [Rethinking External Communication of Autonomous Vehicles: Is the Field Converging, Diverging, or Stalling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06278)
*Tram Thi Minh Tran,Debargha Dey,Martin Tomitsch*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 对620篇自动驾驶车辆外部人机界面研究的综合分析显示：该领域在安全优先的简单视觉提示上趋于收敛，但在必要性和实施方式上持续分歧，形成了从广泛研究探索到有限部署应用的过滤漏斗模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动驾驶车辆进入公共空间，外部人机界面被提出用于与外部道路使用者沟通。尽管已有十年研究和数百项研究，但尚不清楚该领域是在共享原则上趋同还是在方法上分歧，需要系统分析来追踪研究演变和识别收敛趋势。

Method: 对620篇出版物进行多维度分析，辅以行业部署和监管文件分析，追踪研究演变并识别收敛模式。

Result: 分析揭示了三个主要模式：1)在安全优先核心上收敛——采用简单视觉提示澄清意图；2)在必要性和实施方式上持续分歧；3)渐进过滤漏斗模式——从广泛的研究探索逐步缩小到有限的部署应用，最终被监管规范为最小许可信号集。

Conclusion: 未来研究重点应从开发新原型转向：巩固证据基础、澄清争议点、开发能够适应不同情境的框架，以支持该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: As autonomous vehicles enter public spaces, external human-machine interfaces are proposed to support communication with external road users. A decade of research has produced hundreds of studies and reviews, yet it remains unclear whether the field is converging on shared principles or diverging across approaches. We present a multi-dimensional analysis of 620 publications, complemented by industry deployments and regulatory documents, to track research evolution and identify convergence. The analysis reveals several field-level patterns. First, convergence on a safety-first core: simple visual cues that clarify intent. Second, sustained divergence in necessity and implementation. Third, a progressive filtering funnel: broad exploration in research and concepts narrows in deployment and is codified by regulation into a minimal set of permitted signals. These insights point to a shift in emphasis for future work, from producing new prototypes toward consolidating evidence, clarifying points of contention, and developing frameworks that can adapt across contexts.

</details>


### [121] [Reimagining Legal Fact Verification with GenAI: Toward Effective Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06305)
*Sirui Han,Yuyao Zhang,Yidan Huang,Xueyan Li,Chengzhong Liu,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨生成式AI在法律事实核查中的应用，通过访谈律师了解当前实践、采用障碍及未来系统设计需求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律实践中的事实核查是关键但研究不足的领域，现有研究关注法律工作流自动化和高风险领域的人机协作，但对生成式AI如何支持需要审慎判断和严格问责的事实核查任务了解有限

Method: 采用半结构化访谈法，对18名律师进行访谈，了解他们当前的事实核查实践、对生成式AI采用的态度以及对未来系统的期望

Result: 律师目前将生成式AI用于低风险任务（如起草和语言优化），但对准确性、保密性和责任问题的担忧限制了其在事实核查中的应用；这些担忧转化为对可信赖和可问责AI系统的核心设计要求

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，提出了法律事实核查中人机协作的设计见解，强调开发可审计的系统，在效率与专业判断之间取得平衡，并在高风险实践中维护伦理和法律问责

Abstract: Fact verification is a critical yet underexplored component of non-litigation legal practice. While existing research has examined automation in legal workflow and human-AI collaboration in high-stakes domains, little is known about how GenAI can support fact verification, a task that demands prudent judgment and strict accountability. To address this, we conducted semi-structured interviews with 18 lawyers to understand their current verification practices, attitudes toward GenAI adoption, and expectations for future systems. We found that while lawyers use GenAI for low-risk tasks like drafting and language optimization, concerns over accuracy, confidentiality, and liability are currently limiting its adoption for fact verification. These concerns translate into core design requirements for AI systems that are trustworthy and accountable. Based on these, we contribute design insights for human-AI collaboration in legal fact verification, emphasizing the development of auditable systems that balance efficiency with professional judgment and uphold ethical and legal accountability in high-stakes practice.

</details>


### [122] [How Do Human Creators Embrace Human-AI Co-Creation? A Perspective on Human Agency of Screenwriters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06327)
*Yuying Tang,Jiayi Zhou,Haotian Li,Xing Xie,Xiaojuan Ma,Huamin Qu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过两周对19位专业编剧的调研，基于班杜拉的人类能动性理论，探讨编剧如何将AI融入创作过程，发现他们通过实践反思发展了自身和人类-AI协同创作范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注人类-AI协同创作的特定时刻快照，较少关注人类如何逐步动员、调节和反思以形成实践。基于班杜拉的人类能动性理论，研究旨在深入理解专业编剧如何将AI融入创作过程。

Method: 采用为期两周的研究，招募19位专业编剧，基于班杜拉的人类能动性理论框架，调查他们如何将AI融入创作过程，关注他们的规划、预见、响应和实践反思。

Result: 研究发现编剧不仅能有意识地规划、预见和响应AI使用，更重要的是通过实践反思发展了自身和人类-AI协同创作范式（包括认知、策略和工作流程）。他们还表达了对未来AI更好支持其能动性的各种期望。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，论文为编剧、工具开发者和研究者提供了广泛的讨论和可操作建议，以促进可持续的人类-AI协同创作。

Abstract: Generative AI has greatly transformed creative work in various domains, such as screenwriting. To understand this transformation, prior research often focused on capturing a snapshot of human-AI co-creation practice at a specific moment, with less attention to how humans mobilize, regulate, and reflect to form the practice gradually. Motivated by Bandura's theory of human agency, we conducted a two-week study with 19 professional screenwriters to investigate how they embraced AI in their creation process. Our findings revealed that screenwriters not only mindfully planned, foresaw, and responded to AI usage, but, more importantly, through reflections on practice, they developed themselves and human-AI co-creation paradigms, such as cognition, strategies, and workflows. They also expressed various expectations for how future AI should better support their agency. Based on our findings, we conclude this paper with extensive discussion and actionable suggestions to screenwriters, tool developers, and researchers for sustainable human-AI co-creation.

</details>


### [123] [Simulating Word Suggestion Usage in Mobile Typing to Guide Intelligent Text Entry Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06489)
*Yang Li,Anna Maria Feit*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: WSTypist是一个基于强化学习的模型，用于模拟打字者如何将单词建议整合到打字过程中，通过认知机制建模来减少长期用户研究的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能文本输入方法（如单词建议）在移动打字中广泛应用，但改进这些系统面临挑战：建议使用的认知机制理解不足，且评估新系统通常需要长期用户研究来考虑行为适应。

Method: WSTypist基于强化学习，建立在最近的分层控制打字模型基础上，专注于模拟打字者整合单词建议的高层决策认知机制：评估效率增益、考虑拼写不确定性、包含对AI支持的个体依赖。

Result: WSTypist能够模拟多样化的人类建议使用策略，重现个体差异，并在不同系统间具有泛化能力。在四个设计案例中，展示了计算理性模型如何通过模拟用户对UI或算法变化的适应来支持设计过程中的假设分析。

Conclusion: 计算理性模型如WSTypist能够模拟用户如何适应界面和算法变化，为设计过程提供假设分析，从而减少对长期用户研究的依赖，促进智能文本输入系统的改进。

Abstract: Intelligent text entry (ITE) methods, such as word suggestions, are widely used in mobile typing, yet improving ITE systems is challenging because the cognitive mechanisms behind suggestion use remain poorly understood, and evaluating new systems often requires long-term user studies to account for behavioral adaptation. We present WSTypist, a reinforcement learning-based model that simulates how typists integrate word suggestions into typing. It builds on recent hierarchical control models of typing, but focuses on the cognitive mechanisms that underlie the high-level decision-making for effectively integrating word suggestions into manual typing: assessing efficiency gains, considering orthographic uncertainties, and including personal reliance on AI support. Our evaluations show that WSTypist simulates diverse human-like suggestion-use strategies, reproduces individual differences, and generalizes across different systems. Importantly, we demonstrate on four design cases how computational rationality models can be used to inform what-if analyses during the design process, by simulating how users might adapt to changes in the UI or in the algorithmic support, reducing the need for long-term user studies.

</details>


### [124] [Personality as Relational Infrastructure: User Perceptions of Personality-Trait-Infused LLM Messaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06596)
*Dominik P. Hofer,David Haag,Rania Islambouli,Jan D. Smeddinck*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索LLM生成的行为改变消息中人格特质个性化是否通过单条消息优化还是通过累积暴露模式影响用户感知，发现在消息层面无关联，但在人格信息消息比例更高的参与者中感知更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字行为改变系统依赖系统发起的重复消息支持用户，LLM使消息能在多次交互中保持个性化一致性，但尚不清楚这种个性化是通过改进单条消息还是通过累积暴露模式塑造用户感知。

Method: 在LLM生成的JITAIs背景下，采用对照回顾性研究，90名参与者评估四种LLM策略生成的消息：基线提示、少样本提示、微调模型和检索增强生成，每种策略都包含有无大五人格特质的人格对齐通信。使用序数多水平模型进行内-间分解，区分试验水平效应（人格信息是否改进单条消息评价）和个人水平暴露效应（接收更高比例人格信息消息的参与者是否表现出系统性不同的整体感知）。

Result: 结果显示无试验水平关联，但接收更高比例大五人格特质信息消息的参与者将消息评价为更个性化、更合适，并报告更少的负面情绪。使用沟通适应理论进行事后分析。

Conclusion: 行为改变系统中基于人格的个性化可能主要通过累积暴露而非单条消息优化发挥作用，这对自适应系统在持续人机交互中的设计和评估具有启示意义。需要在真实世界环境中进行现场纵向研究验证这些发现。

Abstract: Digital behaviour change systems increasingly rely on repeated, system-initiated messages to support users in everyday contexts. LLMs enable these messages to be personalised consistently across interactions, yet it remains unclear whether such personalisation improves individual messages or instead shapes users' perceptions through patterns of exposure. We explore this question in the context of LLM-generated JITAIs, which are short, context-aware messages delivered at moments deemed appropriate to support behaviour change, using physical activity as an application domain. In a controlled retrospective study, 90 participants evaluated messages generated using four LLM strategies: baseline prompting, few-shot prompting, fine-tuned models, and retrieval augmented generation, each implemented with and without Big Five Personality Traits to produce personality-aligned communication across multiple scenarios. Using ordinal multilevel models with within-between decomposition, we distinguish trial-level effects, whether personality information improves evaluations of individual messages, from person-level exposure effects, whether participants receiving higher proportions of personality-informed messages exhibit systematically different overall perceptions. Results showed no trial-level associations, but participants who received higher proportions of BFPT-informed messages rated the messages as more personalised, appropriate, and reported less negative affect. We use Communication Accommodation Theory for post-hoc analysis. These results suggest that personality-based personalisation in behaviour change systems may operate primarily through aggregate exposure rather than per-message optimisation, with implications for how adaptive systems are designed and evaluated in sustained human-AI interaction. In-situ longitudinal studies are needed to validate these findings in real-world contexts.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond Judgment: Exploring LLM as a Support System for Maternal Mental Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06678)
*Shayla Sharmin,Sadia Afrin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨母亲如何使用大语言模型（LLMs）作为非评判性资源获取情感支持和情境安慰，避免社会评判，重点关注育儿决策、母性内疚和深夜照护等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究关注LLMs在医疗建议和信息提供方面的应用，但母亲如何利用这些工具获取情感和信息支持以避免社会评判的研究仍不足。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探索母亲将LLMs作为非评判性资源的使用模式。

Method: 采用为期10天的混合方法探索性调查，样本量为107名母亲，结合定量和定性方法分析母亲使用LLMs获取情感支持和情境安慰的行为模式。

Result: 研究发现母亲向LLMs提出各类育儿问题以获得自我安慰和避免评判，特别是在育儿决策、母性内疚和深夜照护方面。开放式回答显示母亲对LLMs感到舒适，因为无需考虑社会后果或评判。超过半数参与者更重视人类温暖，但仍有显著少数（尤其是生活在联合家庭中的母亲）选择LLMs以避免人类评判。

Conclusion: LLMs应被定位为低风险互动支持工具而非人类支持的替代品。研究强调了社会情境在塑造情感技术使用中的重要作用，为设计更有效的母亲支持系统提供了见解。

Abstract: In the age of Large Language Models (LLMs), much work has already been done on how LLMs support medication advice and serve as information providers; however, how mothers use these tools for emotional and informational support to avoid social judgment remains underexplored. In this study, we have conducted a 10-day mixed-methods exploratory survey (N=107) to investigate how mothers use LLMs as a non-judgmental resource for emotional support and regulation, as well as situational reassurance. Our findings show that mothers are asking LLMs various questions about childcare to reassure themselves and avoid judgment, particularly around childcare decisions, maternal guilt, and late-night caregiving. Open-ended responses also show that mothers are comfortable with LLMs because they do not have to think about social consequences or judgment. Although they use LLMs for quick information or reassurance to avoid judgment, the results also show that more than half of the participants value human warmth over LLMs; however, a significant minority, especially those who live in a joint family, consider LLMs to avoid human judgment. These findings help us understand how we can frame LLMs as low-risk interaction support rather than as a replacement for human support, and highlight the role of social context in shaping emotional technology use.

</details>


### [126] [PrefIx: Understand and Adapt to User Preference in Human-Agent Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06714)
*Jialin Li,Zhenhao Chen,Hanjun Luo,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Prefix是一个可配置的环境，用于评估LLM智能体的任务完成度和交互质量，通过Interaction-as-a-Tool范式将交互行为结构化，并引入用户体验作为核心指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基准测试只关注任务准确性，忽略了智能体的交互模式（如过度确认、不透明推理、节奏不匹配等），这些不良交互模式会降低用户体验，即使任务完成正确也会让用户感到沮丧。

Method: 提出Prefix可配置环境，采用Interaction-as-a-Tool范式将交互行为视为结构化工具调用；定义31个偏好设置和14个属性；将用户体验作为核心指标；使用复合LLM-as-a-Judge机制在七个UX维度进行评估。

Result: 评估系统表现出强聚合可靠性（ICC > 0.79）、高内部一致性（alpha = 0.943）和良好的人类相关性（rho = 0.52-0.78）；偏好感知智能体显示平均UX提升7.6%，偏好对齐度提高18.5%。

Conclusion: Prefix环境成功填补了当前评估框架的空白，不仅评估任务准确性，还系统评估交互质量，为开发更用户友好的LLM智能体提供了重要工具和评估标准。

Abstract: LLM-based agents can complete tasks correctly yet still frustrate users through poor interaction patterns, such as excessive confirmations, opaque reasoning, or misaligned pacing. Current benchmarks evaluate task accuracy but overlook how agents interact: whether they infer preferences from implicit cues, adapt dynamically, or maintain fine-grained interaction quality. We introduce Prefix, a configurable environment that evaluates both what agents accomplish and how they interact. Central to Prefix is the Interaction-as-a-Tool (IaaT) paradigm, which treats interaction behaviors as structured tool calls, unifying them with existing evaluation frameworks. We define 31 preference settings across 14 attributes and formalize user experience (UX) as a core metric alongside task accuracy. A composite LLM-as-a-Judge mechanism across seven UX dimensions achieves strong aggregate reliability (ICC > 0.79), high internal consistency (alpha = 0.943), and human correlation (rho = 0.52-0.78). Preference-aware agents show 7.6% average UX improvement and 18.5% gain in preference alignment. Our work is openly accessible.

</details>


### [127] [Redundant is Not Redundant: Automating Efficient Categorical Palette Design Unifying Color & Shape Encodings with CatPAW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06792)
*Chin Tseng,Arran Zeyu Wang,Ghulam Jilani Quadri,Danielle Albers Szafir*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过四个众包实验评估了颜色-形状冗余编码在多类别散点图中的效果，发现冗余编码能显著提高类别相关性评估的准确性，特别是在5-8个类别时效果最明显，并开发了基于实证的调色板设计工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多类别散点图中，设计师常结合颜色和形状通道创建冗余编码以增强类别区分，但关于冗余编码有效性的证据存在矛盾，且缺乏构建有效组合的指导原则。

Method: 通过四个众包实验系统评估颜色-形状冗余编码，识别不同类别数量下的高性能配置，分析颜色与形状之间的交互效应。

Result: 冗余编码显著提高了类别级相关性评估的准确性，在5-8个类别时效果最为明显；颜色和形状之间存在显著的交互效应，需要仔细配对；基于研究发现开发了实证基础的调色板设计工具。

Conclusion: 该研究通过系统识别有效的颜色-形状冗余组合并将这些见解嵌入实用的调色板设计工具，推进了对数据可视化中类别感知的理解，为设计师提供了基于实证的指导。

Abstract: Colors and shapes are commonly used to encode categories in multi-class scatterplots. Designers often combine the two channels to create redundant encodings, aiming to enhance class distinctions. However, evidence for the effectiveness of redundancy remains conflicted, and guidelines for constructing effective combinations are limited. This paper presents four crowdsourced experiments evaluating redundant color-shape encodings and identifying high-performing configurations across different category numbers. Results show that redundancy significantly improves accuracy in assessing class-level correlations, with the strongest benefits for 5-8 categories. We also find pronounced interaction effects between colors and shapes, underscoring the need for careful pairing in designing redundant encodings. Drawing on these findings, we introduce a categorical palette design tool that enables designers to construct empirically grounded palettes for effective categorical visualization. Our work advances understanding of categorical perception in data visualization by systematically identifying effective redundant color-shape combinations and embedding these insights into a practical palette design tool.

</details>


### [128] [Directing Space: Rehearsing Architecture as Performer with Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06915)
*Pavlos Panagiotidis,Jocelyn Spence,Nils Jaeger,Paul Tennent*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发用于现场表演的AI中介互动架构系统，将互动架构视为表演性智能体，通过LLM解释空间行为和语音信号，生成实时环境适应，支持艺术家与AI在排练中的创造性对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地嵌入交互式和沉浸式艺术环境，艺术家和技术人员发现利用AI的解释性和自主能力作为现场表演中创造性合作者的新机会。需要建立概念和技术基础，使表演创作者与互动架构能够在排练环境中协作。

Method: 提出面向排练的原型系统，将互动架构视为表演性智能体，感知空间行为和语音信号，通过大型语言模型解释这些信号，并生成实时环境适应。系统采用虚拟蓝图支持迭代实验，使用推理轨迹来指导基于戏剧原则的建筑交互设计。

Result: 这是一个进行中的工作，提出了概念框架和原型系统设计，旨在支持艺术家与AI智能体之间的创造性对话，通过推理轨迹为基于戏剧原则的建筑交互设计提供信息。

Conclusion: 该研究为AI中介环境在创造性实践中的塑造和测试提供了排练导向的方法，建立了表演创作者与互动架构协作的基础，强调在物理表演空间中部署，支持艺术家与AI智能体之间的迭代实验和创造性对话。

Abstract: As AI systems increasingly become embedded in interactive and im-mersive artistic environments, artists and technologists are discovering new opportunities to engage with their interpretive and autonomous capacities as creative collaborators in live performance. The focus of this work-in-progress is on outlining conceptual and technical foundations under which performance-makers and interactive architecture can collaborate within rehearsal settings. It introduces a rehearsal-oriented prototype system for shaping and testing AI-mediated environments within creative practice. This approach treats interactive architecture as a performative agent that senses spatial behaviour and speech, interprets these signals through a large language model, and generates real-time environmental adaptations. Designed for deployment in physical performance spaces, the system employs virtual blueprints to support iterative experimentation and creative dialogue between artists and AI agents, using reasoning traces to inform architectural interaction design grounded in dramaturgical principles.

</details>


### [129] [Understanding Workplace Relatedness Support among Healthcare Professionals: A Four-Layer Model and Implications for Technology Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06916)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Dorian Peters,Lan Xiao,Jingjing Sun,Laura Moradbakhti,Andrew Hall,Rafael A. Calvo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过访谈和协同设计工作坊探索了医疗保健专业人员（HCPs）在职场中的关联性需求，并提出了四层模型和八个设计概念来支持HCPs的心理健康和韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗保健专业人员面临日益增加的职业压力和倦怠，支持他们的关联性需求对其心理健康和韧性至关重要。然而，技术如何支持HCPs在工作场所的关联性需求尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈（n=15）和协同设计工作坊（n=21）与英国国家医疗服务体系（NHS）的HCPs合作，探索他们当前的工作实践和对职场关联性支持的偏好，以及如何利用技术来促进关联性。

Result: 定性分析得出了HCPs关联性需求的四层模型：非正式互动、同志情谊与纽带、社区与组织关怀、共享身份。工作坊生成了八个设计概念（如趣味相遇、协同行动、记忆与故事等），这些概念将四层关联性需求具体化。

Conclusion: 研究强调了理论相关性、实际设计意义，以及在数字化和人工智能时代加强HCPs关联性支持的必要性。

Abstract: Healthcare professionals (HCPs) face increasing occupational stress and burnout. Supporting HCPs need for relatedness is fundamental to their psychological wellbeing and resilience. However, how technologies could support HCPs relatedness in the workplace remains less explored. This study incorporated semi-structured interviews (n = 15) and co-design workshops (n = 21) with HCPs working in the UK National Health Service (NHS), to explore their current practices and preferences for workplace relatedness support, and how technology could be utilized to benefit relatedness. Qualitative analysis yielded a four-layer model of HCPs relatedness need, which includes Informal Interactions, Camaraderie and Bond, Community and Organizational Care, and Shared Identity. Workshops generated eight design concepts (e.g., Playful Encounter, Collocated Action, and Memories and Stories) that operationalize the four relatedness need layers. We conclude by highlighting the theoretical relevance, practical design implications, and the necessity to strengthen relatedness support for HCPs in the era of digitalization and artificial intelligence.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [NanoNet: Parameter-Efficient Learning with Label-Scarce Supervision for Lightweight Text Mining Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06093)
*Qianren Mao,Yashuo Luo,Ziqi Qin,Junnan Liu,Weifeng Jiang,Zhijun Chen,Zhuoran Li,Likang Xiao,Chuou Xu,Qili Zhang,Hanwen Hao,Jingzheng Li,Chunghua Lin,Jianxin Li,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NanoNet框架，通过参数高效学习实现轻量级文本挖掘，在有限监督下结合在线知识蒸馏和互学习正则化来提升小模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级半监督学习策略虽然能节省标注样本和降低推理成本，但存在计算密集、易陷入局部最优的问题。研究者希望探索在有限标注监督、轻量级微调和快速推理小模型这三个低成本场景下进行文本挖掘的可行性

Method: 提出NanoNet框架：1) 采用参数高效学习减少训练成本；2) 使用在线知识蒸馏生成多个小模型；3) 通过互学习正则化增强模型性能；4) 整个流程在有限监督下进行，最终得到轻量级下游推理模型

Result: 该框架能够降低训练成本，减少监督需求，最终获得适用于下游任务的轻量级模型

Conclusion: NanoNet为轻量级文本挖掘提供了一种有效的解决方案，通过参数高效学习和互学习正则化，在有限监督下实现了低成本、高性能的小模型训练

Abstract: The lightweight semi-supervised learning (LSL) strategy provides an effective approach of conserving labeled samples and minimizing model inference costs. Prior research has effectively applied knowledge transfer learning and co-training regularization from large to small models in LSL. However, such training strategies are computationally intensive and prone to local optima, thereby increasing the difficulty of finding the optimal solution. This has prompted us to investigate the feasibility of integrating three low-cost scenarios for text mining tasks: limited labeled supervision, lightweight fine-tuning, and rapid-inference small models. We propose NanoNet, a novel framework for lightweight text mining that implements parameter-efficient learning with limited supervision. It employs online knowledge distillation to generate multiple small models and enhances their performance through mutual learning regularization. The entire process leverages parameter-efficient learning, reducing training costs and minimizing supervision requirements, ultimately yielding a lightweight model for downstream inference.

</details>


### [131] [Agentic Workflow Using RBA$_θ$ for Event Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06097)
*Purbak Sengupta,Sambeet Mishra,Sonal Shreya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出事件优先、频率感知的风电爬坡事件预测范式，直接预测爬坡事件并重建功率轨迹，而非从密集预测中推断事件


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电爬坡事件因强变异性、多尺度动力学和特定场地气象效应而难以预测，传统方法在跨场地泛化方面有限

Method: 基于增强的RBAθ方法的事件表示，渐进整合统计、机器学习和深度学习模型；引入事件优先的深度架构，集成小波频率分解、时间激励特征和自适应特征选择；提出智能预测层动态选择工作流

Result: 实现稳定的长时域事件预测、物理一致的轨迹重建和零样本迁移到未见风电场；爬坡幅度和持续时间受不同中频带控制，可从稀疏事件预测中准确重建信号

Conclusion: 事件优先、频率感知的预测为轨迹优先的风电预测提供了可迁移且操作对齐的替代方案

Abstract: Wind power ramp events are difficult to forecast due to strong variability, multi-scale dynamics, and site-specific meteorological effects. This paper proposes an event-first, frequency-aware forecasting paradigm that directly predicts ramp events and reconstructs the power trajectory thereafter, rather than inferring events from dense forecasts. The framework is built on an enhanced Ramping Behaviour Analysis (RBA$_θ$) method's event representation and progressively integrates statistical, machine-learning, and deep-learning models. Traditional forecasting models with post-hoc event extraction provides a strong interpretable baseline but exhibits limited generalisation across sites. Direct event prediction using Random Forests improves robustness over survival-based formulations, motivating fully event-aware modelling. To capture the multi-scale nature of wind ramps, we introduce an event-first deep architecture that integrates wavelet-based frequency decomposition, temporal excitation features, and adaptive feature selection. The resulting sequence models enable stable long-horizon event prediction, physically consistent trajectory reconstruction, and zero-shot transfer to previously unseen wind farms. Empirical analysis shows that ramp magnitude and duration are governed by distinct mid-frequency bands, allowing accurate signal reconstruction from sparse event forecasts. An agentic forecasting layer is proposed, in which specialised workflows are selected dynamically based on operational context. Together, the framework demonstrates that event-first, frequency-aware forecasting provides a transferable and operationally aligned alternative to trajectory-first wind-power prediction.

</details>


### [132] [Toward Faithful and Complete Answer Construction from a Single Document](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06103)
*Zhaoyang Chen,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EVE是一个结构化框架，通过分解为提取、验证和枚举三个步骤来解决LLM在文档推理中存在的完整性和忠实性问题，显著提升了召回率、精确率和F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大型语言模型基于统计的下一个词预测机制，偏向高概率延续而非基于源内容的详尽忠实回答，缺乏确保完整性和忠实性的系统机制，这与AI安全原则存在根本冲突。

Method: EVE是一个结构化框架，将高严谨推理分解为提取、验证和枚举三个步骤，通过约束生成到可验证的管道中，不同于自由形式的提示方法。

Result: EVE在召回率、精确率和F1分数上实现了同步一致提升：召回率和精确率分别提升高达24%和29%，F1分数相应提升31%，打破了单次LLM生成中覆盖率和准确性之间的长期权衡，同时缓解了长度限制导致的生成截断问题。

Conclusion: EVE框架有效解决了LLM在文档推理中的完整性和忠实性问题，但受自然语言固有模糊性的限制，表现出性能饱和现象，反映了基于语言推理的基本限制。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) are powerful generators driven by statistical next-token prediction. While effective at producing fluent text, this design biases models toward high-probability continuations rather than exhaustive and faithful answers grounded in source content. As a result, directly applying LLMs lacks systematic mechanisms to ensure both completeness (avoiding omissions) and faithfulness (avoiding unsupported content), which fundamentally conflicts with core AI safety principles. To address this limitation, we present EVE, a structured framework for document-grounded reasoning.
  Unlike free-form prompting, EVE constrains generation to a structured, verifiable pipeline that decomposes high-rigor reasoning into extraction, validation, and enumeration. Empirically, this design enables consistent and simultaneous improvements in recall, precision, and F1-score: recall and precision increase by up to 24\% and 29\%, respectively, with a corresponding 31\% gain in F1-score. This effectively breaks the long-standing trade-off between coverage and accuracy typical of single-pass LLM generation, while also mitigating generation truncation caused by length limitations. At the same time, we emphasize that EVE exhibits performance saturation due to the inherent ambiguity of natural language, reflecting fundamental limits of language-based reasoning.

</details>


### [133] [Pragmatic Curiosity: A Hybrid Learning-Optimization Paradigm via Active Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06104)
*Yingke Li,Anjali Parashar,Enlu Zhou,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"实用好奇心"混合学习-优化范式，基于主动推理，通过最小化期望自由能来平衡实用效用与认知信息增益，在多种现实混合任务中优于传统贝叶斯优化和实验设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多工程和科学工作流程依赖昂贵的黑盒评估，需要同时改进性能和减少不确定性的决策。传统的贝叶斯优化(BO)和贝叶斯实验设计(BED)分别处理目标寻求和信息寻求，对学习和优化内在耦合的混合场景指导有限。

Method: 提出"实用好奇心"混合学习-优化范式，源自主动推理理论。该方法通过最小化期望自由能来选择行动，这一单一目标将实用效用与认知信息增益耦合起来。

Result: 在多种现实混合任务中验证了实用好奇心的有效性，包括约束系统辨识、目标主动搜索和未知偏好的复合优化。在所有基准测试中，实用好奇心始终优于强基线方法，实现了更高的估计精度、更好的关键区域覆盖和更优的最终解质量。

Conclusion: 实用好奇心为混合学习-优化问题提供了统一框架，有效平衡了目标寻求和信息寻求，在多种实际应用中展现出优于传统方法的性能。

Abstract: Many engineering and scientific workflows depend on expensive black-box evaluations, requiring decision-making that simultaneously improves performance and reduces uncertainty. Bayesian optimization (BO) and Bayesian experimental design (BED) offer powerful yet largely separate treatments of goal-seeking and information-seeking, providing limited guidance for hybrid settings where learning and optimization are intrinsically coupled. We propose "pragmatic curiosity," a hybrid learning-optimization paradigm derived from active inference, in which actions are selected by minimizing the expected free energy--a single objective that couples pragmatic utility with epistemic information gain. We demonstrate the practical effectiveness and flexibility of pragmatic curiosity on various real-world hybrid tasks, including constrained system identification, targeted active search, and composite optimization with unknown preferences. Across these benchmarks, pragmatic curiosity consistently outperforms strong BO-type and BED-type baselines, delivering higher estimation accuracy, better critical-region coverage, and improved final solution quality.

</details>


### [134] [Compressing LLMs with MoP: Mixture of Pruners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06127)
*Bruno Lopes Yamamoto,Lucas Lauton de Alcantara,Victor Zacarias,Leandro Giusti Mugnaini,Keith Ando Ogawa,Lucas Pellicer,Rosimeire Pereira Costa,Edson Bollis,Anna Helena Reali Costa,Artur Jordao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoP是一种统一深度和宽度剪枝的迭代框架，在LLaMA系列模型上实现了结构化剪枝的新突破，显著减少延迟并保持准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的高计算需求促使参数减少和推理加速方法的发展，现有剪枝方法通常只关注单一维度（深度或宽度），缺乏统一框架

Method: MoP（Mixture of Pruners）是一个迭代框架，每轮生成深度剪枝和宽度剪枝两个分支，选择最佳候选推进剪枝路径，统一了深度和宽度剪枝维度

Result: 在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上超越了现有剪枝方法，在广泛压缩范围内保持更高准确性；40%压缩时端到端延迟减少39%；在视觉语言模型LLaVA-1.5上显著提升计算效率，仅文本恢复微调即可恢复视觉任务性能

Conclusion: MoP通过统一深度和宽度剪枝维度，实现了结构化剪枝的新突破，为大型语言模型和视觉语言模型的高效部署提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: The high computational demands of Large Language Models (LLMs) motivate methods that reduce parameter count and accelerate inference. In response, model pruning emerges as an effective strategy, yet current methods typically focus on a single dimension-depth or width. We introduce MoP (Mixture of Pruners), an iterative framework that unifies these dimensions. At each iteration, MoP generates two branches-pruning in depth versus pruning in width-and selects a candidate to advance the path. On LLaMA-2 and LLaMA-3, MoP advances the frontier of structured pruning, exceeding the accuracy of competing methods across a broad set of compression regimes. It also consistently outperforms depth-only and width-only pruning. Furthermore, MoP translates structural pruning into real speedup, reducing end-to-end latency by 39% at 40% compression. Finally, extending MoP to the vision-language model LLaVA-1.5, we notably improve computational efficiency and demonstrate that text-only recovery fine-tuning can restore performance even on visual tasks.

</details>


### [135] [Continuous-time reinforcement learning: ellipticity enables model-free value function approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06930)
*Wenlong Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究连续时间马尔可夫扩散过程的离策略强化学习，提出基于函数逼近的Sobolev-prox fitted q-learning算法，证明在椭圆性条件下强化学习难度不高于监督学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究连续时间马尔可夫扩散过程的离策略强化学习问题，针对离散时间观测和动作的情况，旨在开发无需对动态过程做不现实结构假设的模型无关算法。

Method: 利用扩散过程的椭圆性建立Bellman算子的Hilbert空间正定性和有界性，提出Sobolev-prox fitted q-learning算法，通过迭代求解最小二乘回归问题学习价值和优势函数。

Result: 建立了估计误差的oracle不等式，误差由函数类的最佳逼近误差、局部复杂度、指数衰减的优化误差和数值离散化误差控制，证明椭圆性使得扩散过程的强化学习难度不高于监督学习。

Conclusion: 椭圆性是马尔可夫扩散过程强化学习的关键结构性质，在此条件下，基于函数逼近的强化学习问题在理论难度上不高于监督学习问题。

Abstract: We study off-policy reinforcement learning for controlling continuous-time Markov diffusion processes with discrete-time observations and actions. We consider model-free algorithms with function approximation that learn value and advantage functions directly from data, without unrealistic structural assumptions on the dynamics.
  Leveraging the ellipticity of the diffusions, we establish a new class of Hilbert-space positive definiteness and boundedness properties for the Bellman operators. Based on these properties, we propose the Sobolev-prox fitted $q$-learning algorithm, which learns value and advantage functions by iteratively solving least-squares regression problems. We derive oracle inequalities for the estimation error, governed by (i) the best approximation error of the function classes, (ii) their localized complexity, (iii) exponentially decaying optimization error, and (iv) numerical discretization error. These results identify ellipticity as a key structural property that renders reinforcement learning with function approximation for Markov diffusions no harder than supervised learning.

</details>


### [136] [Tempora: Characterising the Time-Contingent Utility of Online Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06136)
*Sudarshan Sreeram,Young D. Kwon,Cecilia Mascolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tempora框架首次系统评估测试时适应(TTA)在时间压力下的表现，揭示传统评估忽略的准确率-延迟权衡，发现方法排名在时间约束下会反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TTA评估假设无限处理时间，忽略了实际部署中的时间压力。随着ML在延迟敏感应用中的普及，预测延迟过高会使其失效，需要评估TTA在时间约束下的表现。

Method: 提出Tempora框架，包含：1)建模部署约束的时间场景；2)操作化测量的评估协议；3)量化准确率-延迟权衡的时间相关效用指标。具体实现三个指标：离散效用(硬截止时间)、连续效用(延迟衰减价值)、摊销效用(预算约束)。

Result: 在ImageNet-C上对7种TTA方法进行240次时间评估，发现：1)传统排名无法预测时间压力下的排名；2)传统最优方法ETA在41.2%评估中表现不佳；3)最佳方法随损坏类型和时间压力变化，无明确优胜者；4)排名反转现象普遍。

Conclusion: Tempora首次系统评估TTA在时间约束下的表现，揭示了传统评估忽略的准确率-延迟权衡，为实践者提供方法选择依据，为研究者提供可部署适应的研究目标。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) offers a compelling remedy for machine learning (ML) models that degrade under domain shifts, improving generalisation on-the-fly with only unlabelled samples. This flexibility suits real deployments, yet conventional evaluations unrealistically assume unbounded processing time, overlooking the accuracy-latency trade-off. As ML increasingly underpins latency-sensitive and user-facing use-cases, temporal pressure constrains the viability of adaptable inference; predictions arriving too late to act on are futile. We introduce Tempora, a framework for evaluating TTA under this pressure. It consists of temporal scenarios that model deployment constraints, evaluation protocols that operationalise measurement, and time-contingent utility metrics that quantify the accuracy-latency trade-off. We instantiate the framework with three such metrics: (1) discrete utility for asynchronous streams with hard deadlines, (2) continuous utility for interactive settings where value decays with latency, and (3) amortised utility for budget-constrained deployments. Applying Tempora to seven TTA methods on ImageNet-C across 240 temporal evaluations reveals rank instability: conventional rankings do not predict rankings under temporal pressure; ETA, a state-of-the-art method in the conventional setting, falls short in 41.2% of evaluations. The highest-utility method varies with corruption type and temporal pressure, with no clear winner. By enabling systematic evaluation across diverse temporal constraints for the first time, Tempora reveals when and why rankings invert, offering practitioners a lens for method selection and researchers a target for deployable adaptation.

</details>


### [137] [Flow Matching for Offline Reinforcement Learning with Discrete Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06138)
*Fairoz Nower Khan,Nabuat Zaman Nahim,Ruiquan Huang,Haibo Yang,Peizhong Ju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将流匹配扩展到离散动作空间的多目标离线强化学习框架，支持多智能体设置，通过动作量化也可应用于连续控制问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散模型和流匹配的生成策略在离线强化学习中表现出色，但主要局限于连续动作空间。需要扩展框架以支持更广泛的离线RL设置，包括离散动作空间和多目标场景。

Method: 1. 用连续时间马尔可夫链替代连续流，使用Q加权的流匹配目标进行训练；2. 扩展到多智能体设置，通过因子化条件路径缓解联合动作空间的指数增长；3. 通过动作量化将离散框架应用于连续控制问题

Result: 理论证明在理想条件下优化该目标可恢复最优策略；实验表明方法在高维控制、多模态决策、动态变化的多目标偏好等实际场景中表现稳健；离散框架通过动作量化在连续控制问题中提供表示复杂性与性能的灵活权衡

Conclusion: 提出了一个通用的流匹配框架，成功将生成策略扩展到离散动作空间和多目标离线强化学习，为更广泛的RL应用场景提供了灵活有效的解决方案

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion models and flow matching have shown strong promise for offline reinforcement learning (RL), but their applicability remains largely confined to continuous action spaces. To address a broader range of offline RL settings, we extend flow matching to a general framework that supports discrete action spaces with multiple objectives. Specifically, we replace continuous flows with continuous-time Markov chains, trained using a Q-weighted flow matching objective. We then extend our design to multi-agent settings, mitigating the exponential growth of joint action spaces via a factorized conditional path. We theoretically show that, under idealized conditions, optimizing this objective recovers the optimal policy. Extensive experiments further demonstrate that our method performs robustly in practical scenarios, including high-dimensional control, multi-modal decision-making, and dynamically changing preferences over multiple objectives. Our discrete framework can also be applied to continuous-control problems through action quantization, providing a flexible trade-off between representational complexity and performance.

</details>


### [138] [Optimistic Training and Convergence of Q-Learning -- Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06146)
*Prashant Mehta,Sean Meyn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析了Q学习在函数逼近下的稳定性问题，指出即使基函数理想（真实Q函数在其张成空间中），投影贝尔曼方程仍可能存在多个解，导致算法不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明，在(ε,κ)-驯服Gibbs策略下，带线性函数逼近的Q学习是稳定的，且投影贝尔曼方程有解。但未解决解的唯一性问题，也未给出标准表格或线性MDP设置之外的收敛准则。本文旨在扩展这些结果，澄清先前工作，并揭示收敛需要更多结构条件。

Method: 通过理论分析和构造反例：1）使用一维示例展示在无意识训练策略下，投影贝尔曼方程可能无解或多解，且算法不稳定；2）构造基函数理想但投影贝尔曼方程在贪婪策略下有两个解的例子，说明即使条件看似良好也可能存在收敛问题。

Result: 1）证明收敛需要比先前假设更强的结构条件；2）即使基函数理想（真实Q函数在其张成空间中），投影贝尔曼方程在贪婪策略下仍可能存在多个解，对于所有足够小的ε>0和κ≥1的(ε,κ)-驯服Gibbs策略也是如此；3）在无意识训练策略下，算法可能不稳定。

Conclusion: Q学习在函数逼近下的收敛性需要比线性函数逼近和特定探索策略更严格的结构条件。即使基函数理想，投影贝尔曼方程的多解性仍可能导致算法不稳定，这揭示了强化学习理论分析中的深层挑战。

Abstract: In recent work it is shown that Q-learning with linear function approximation is stable, in the sense of bounded parameter estimates, under the $(\varepsilon,κ)$-tamed Gibbs policy; $κ$ is inverse temperature, and $\varepsilon>0$ is introduced for additional exploration. Under these assumptions it also follows that there is a solution to the projected Bellman equation (PBE). Left open is uniqueness of the solution, and criteria for convergence outside of the standard tabular or linear MDP settings.
  The present work extends these results to other variants of Q-learning, and clarifies prior work: a one dimensional example shows that under an oblivious policy for training there may be no solution to the PBE, or multiple solutions, and in each case the algorithm is not stable under oblivious training.
  The main contribution is that far more structure is required for convergence. An example is presented for which the basis is ideal, in the sense that the true Q-function is in the span of the basis. However, there are two solutions to the PBE under the greedy policy, and hence also for the $(\varepsilon,κ)$-tamed Gibbs policy for all sufficiently small $\varepsilon>0$ and $κ\ge 1$.

</details>


### [139] [MoSE: Mixture of Slimmable Experts for Efficient and Adaptive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06154)
*Nurbek Tastan,Stefanos Laskaridis,Karthik Nandakumar,Samuel Horvath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MoSE架构，在MoE基础上为每个专家引入可调节宽度的嵌套结构，实现更连续的精度-计算量权衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE模型在专家选择后完全执行，精度-计算量权衡存在较大不连续性，需要更灵活的推理时计算控制

Method: 为MoE中的每个专家设计可调节宽度的嵌套结构，结合多宽度训练和标准MoE目标，提出运行时宽度确定策略

Result: MoSE在GPT模型上匹配或超越标准MoE，显著改善精度-计算量Pareto前沿，以更少FLOPs获得相当性能

Conclusion: MoSE通过专家宽度调节实现了更连续的精度-计算量权衡，为大型语言模型提供了更灵活的推理时计算控制

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale large language models efficiently by sparsely activating experts, but once an expert is selected, it is executed fully. Hence, the trade-off between accuracy and computation in an MoE model typically exhibits large discontinuities. We propose Mixture of Slimmable Experts (MoSE), an MoE architecture in which each expert has a nested, slimmable structure that can be executed at variable widths. This enables conditional computation not only over which experts are activated, but also over how much of each expert is utilized. Consequently, a single pretrained MoSE model can support a more continuous spectrum of accuracy-compute trade-offs at inference time. We present a simple and stable training recipe for slimmable experts under sparse routing, combining multi-width training with standard MoE objectives. During inference, we explore strategies for runtime width determination, including a lightweight test-time training mechanism that learns how to map router confidence/probabilities to expert widths under a fixed budget. Experiments on GPT models trained on OpenWebText demonstrate that MoSE matches or improves upon standard MoE at full width and consistently shifts the Pareto frontier for accuracy vs. cost, achieving comparable performance with significantly fewer FLOPs.

</details>


### [140] [Latent Structure Emergence in Diffusion Models via Confidence-Based Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06155)
*Wei Wei,Yizhou Zeng,Kuntian Chen,Sophie Langer,Mariia Seleznova,Hung-Hsu Chou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 扩散模型的初始噪声空间在高置信度样本下展现出明显的类别可分性，而全空间则看似无结构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究扩散模型高维初始噪声空间中是否存在能够预测生成样本类别属性的结构，以及这种结构如何通过分类器置信度分数显现

Method: 通过预训练分类器为生成样本分配置信度分数，分析不同置信度子集中的噪声种子，比较类别可预测性和潜在空间的类别可分性

Result: 初始噪声空间在全噪声实现下看似无结构，但限制在产生高置信度样本的噪声种子时，显示出明显的类别可分性；置信度过滤揭示了仅在特定条件下可观察到的类别相关潜在结构

Conclusion: 扩散模型的潜在空间包含类别相关信息，但仅在通过置信度过滤选择高质量样本时才显现；置信度过滤可作为基于引导方法的替代方案实现条件生成

Abstract: Diffusion models rely on a high-dimensional latent space of initial noise seeds, yet it remains unclear whether this space contains sufficient structure to predict properties of the generated samples, such as their classes. In this work, we investigate the emergence of latent structure through the lens of confidence scores assigned by a pre-trained classifier to generated samples. We show that while the latent space appears largely unstructured when considering all noise realizations, restricting attention to initial noise seeds that produce high-confidence samples reveals pronounced class separability. By comparing class predictability across noise subsets of varying confidence and examining the class separability of the latent space, we find evidence of class-relevant latent structure that becomes observable only under confidence-based filtering. As a practical implication, we discuss how confidence-based filtering enables conditional generation as an alternative to guidance-based methods.

</details>


### [141] [SCONE: A Practical, Constraint-Aware Plug-in for Latent Encoding in Learned DNA Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06157)
*Cihan Ruan,Lebin Zhou,Rongduo Han,Linyi Han,Bingqing Zhao,Chenchen Zhu,Wei Jiang,Wei Wang,Nam Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCONE是一个将神经压缩与DNA编码集成的插件模块，通过四进制算术编码直接在DNA碱基上进行潜在空间编码，动态强制执行生化约束，消除后处理修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前DNA存储与神经压缩管道的集成效率低下。现有方法要么在原始二进制数据上应用冗余约束层（原始但低效），要么将学习的潜在表示通过简单的二进制到四进制转码转换为DNA，丢弃了熵模型的优化。这种不匹配破坏了压缩效率并使编码堆栈复杂化。

Method: SCONE采用四进制算术编码直接在DNA碱基上进行潜在空间编码。其约束感知自适应编码模块动态引导熵编码器的学习概率分布，在编码过程中确定性执行生化约束（GC平衡和同聚物抑制），无需后处理修正。该设计保持完全可逆性，并利用超先验模型的学习先验而无需修改。

Result: SCONE实现了近乎完美的约束满足，计算开销可忽略（<2%延迟），为端到端DNA兼容学习编解码器建立了潜在无关接口。

Conclusion: SCONE通过将潜在压缩和DNA编码合并为单一步骤，解决了神经压缩与DNA存储集成的效率问题，提供了一种高效、约束感知的编码方法，为DNA兼容学习编解码器提供了端到端解决方案。

Abstract: DNA storage has matured from concept to practical stage, yet its integration with neural compression pipelines remains inefficient. Early DNA encoders applied redundancy-heavy constraint layers atop raw binary data - workable but primitive. Recent neural codecs compress data into learned latent representations with rich statistical structure, yet still convert these latents to DNA via naive binary-to-quaternary transcoding, discarding the entropy model's optimization. This mismatch undermines compression efficiency and complicates the encoding stack. A plug-in module that collapses latent compression and DNA encoding into a single step. SCONE performs quaternary arithmetic coding directly on the latent space in DNA bases. Its Constraint-Aware Adaptive Coding module dynamically steers the entropy encoder's learned probability distribution to enforce biochemical constraints - Guanine-Cytosine (GC) balance and homopolymer suppression - deterministically during encoding, eliminating post-hoc correction. The design preserves full reversibility and exploits the hyperprior model's learned priors without modification. Experiments show SCONE achieves near-perfect constraint satisfaction with negligible computational overhead (<2% latency), establishing a latent-agnostic interface for end-to-end DNA-compatible learned codecs.

</details>


### [142] [Statistical Learning from Attribution Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06276)
*Lorne Applebaum,Robert Busa-Fekete,August Y. Chen,Claudio Gentile,Tomer Koren,Aryan Mokhtari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在隐私约束下训练广告转化预测模型的方法，其中点击与转化之间的直接链接不可用，只能通过候选点击集合（归因集）进行学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于隐私保护浏览器API和第三方cookie弃用带来的挑战，在这种环境下，学习者只能观察到点击序列和转化序列，但无法将转化唯一链接到特定点击，只能链接到候选点击集合。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的无偏估计方法，通过归因集构建总体损失的无偏估计器。该方法将问题形式化为从由具有候选先验分布的遗忘对手生成的归因集中学习，并利用经验风险最小化（ERM）实现泛化保证。

Result: 理论分析表明，ERM能够获得与先验信息量成正比的泛化保证，并且对先验估计误差具有鲁棒性。在标准数据集上的简单实证评估显示，该方法显著优于常见的行业启发式方法，特别是在归因集较大或重叠的情况下。

Conclusion: 该研究为隐私约束下的广告转化预测提供了一种有效的无偏学习方法，能够在缺乏明确标签的情况下，通过归因集实现可靠的模型训练，对应对第三方cookie弃用等隐私保护趋势具有实际意义。

Abstract: We address the problem of training conversion prediction models in advertising domains under privacy constraints, where direct links between ad clicks and conversions are unavailable. Motivated by privacy-preserving browser APIs and the deprecation of third-party cookies, we study a setting where the learner observes a sequence of clicks and a sequence of conversions, but can only link a conversion to a set of candidate clicks (an attribution set) rather than a unique source. We formalize this as learning from attribution sets generated by an oblivious adversary equipped with a prior distribution over the candidates. Despite the lack of explicit labels, we construct an unbiased estimator of the population loss from these coarse signals via a novel approach. Leveraging this estimator, we show that Empirical Risk Minimization achieves generalization guarantees that scale with the informativeness of the prior and is also robust against estimation errors in the prior, despite complex dependencies among attribution sets. Simple empirical evaluations on standard datasets suggest our unbiased approach significantly outperforms common industry heuristics, particularly in regimes where attribution sets are large or overlapping.

</details>


### [143] [To 2:4 Sparsity and Beyond: Neuron-level Activation Function to Accelerate LLM Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06183)
*Meghana Madhyastha,Daniel Haziza,Jesse Cai,Newsha Ardalani,Zhiqi Bu,Carole-Jean Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用硬件加速稀疏性加速FFN矩阵乘法，通过稀疏训练与密集训练结合，实现1.4-1.7倍训练加速，保持模型性能不变


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练主要受限于矩阵乘法运算，在Transformer架构中，FFN层占用了大量计算资源（最高可达预训练浮点运算的50%），需要寻找加速方法

Method: 采用硬件加速稀疏性技术：权重使用2:4稀疏模式，激活使用v:n:m（Venom）稀疏模式；结合稀疏训练步骤（加速大部分预训练）和密集训练步骤（训练后期）的混合训练方法

Result: 训练出的模型在质量基准测试中表现相同，端到端训练速度提升1.4-1.7倍；方法适用于A100及以上NVIDIA GPU，与量化等技术正交，也可应用于混合专家架构

Conclusion: 通过硬件加速稀疏性技术有效加速FFN矩阵乘法，实现显著训练加速而不损失模型性能，为大语言模型训练提供了实用的优化方案

Abstract: Trainings of Large Language Models are generally bottlenecked by matrix multiplications. In the Transformer architecture, a large portion of these operations happens in the Feed Forward Network (FFN), and this portion increases for larger models, up to 50% of the total pretraining floating point operations. We show that we can leverage hardware-accelerated sparsity to accelerate all matrix multiplications in the FFN, with 2:4 sparsity for weights and v:n:m (Venom) sparsity for activations. Our recipe relies on sparse training steps to accelerate a large part of the pretraining, associated with regular dense training steps towards the end. Overall, models trained with this approach exhibit the same performance on our quality benchmarks, and can speed up training end-to-end by 1.4 to 1.7x. This approach is applicable to all NVIDIA GPUs starting with the A100 generation, and is orthogonal to common optimization techniques, such as, quantization, and can also be applied to mixture-of-experts model architectures.

</details>


### [144] [$f$-FUM: Federated Unlearning via min--max and $f$-divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06187)
*Radmehr Karimian,Amirhossein Bagheri,Meghdad Kurmanji,Nicholas D. Lane,Gholamali Aminian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新颖的联邦学习遗忘框架，通过最小化优化问题实现高效数据删除，可作为插件应用于各种联邦学习设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习需要在保护隐私的同时满足"被遗忘权"等法律伦理要求，并防御数据投毒攻击，但分布式特性使得删除个体数据贡献变得复杂。

Method: 将联邦遗忘问题表述为最小化优化问题：最大化包含所有数据训练的模型与删除特定数据后重新训练的模型之间的f-散度，同时最小化对保留数据性能的退化。

Result: 该方法相比朴素重新训练实现了显著加速，对模型效用影响最小，且可作为插件应用于几乎所有联邦学习设置，无需服务器端模型退化或特定架构要求。

Conclusion: 提出的联邦遗忘框架为分布式环境下的数据删除提供了高效解决方案，平衡了遗忘效果与模型性能，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for collaborative machine learning across decentralized data sources, preserving privacy by keeping data local. However, increasing legal and ethical demands, such as the "right to be forgotten", and the need to mitigate data poisoning attacks have underscored the urgent necessity for principled data unlearning in FL. Unlike centralized settings, the distributed nature of FL complicates the removal of individual data contributions. In this paper, we propose a novel federated unlearning framework formulated as a min-max optimization problem, where the objective is to maximize an $f$-divergence between the model trained with all data and the model retrained without specific data points, while minimizing the degradation on retained data. Our framework could act like a plugin and be added to almost any federated setup, unlike SOTA methods like (\cite{10269017} which requires model degradation in server, or \cite{khalil2025notfederatedunlearningweight} which requires to involve model architecture and model weights). This formulation allows for efficient approximation of data removal effects in a federated setting. We provide empirical evaluations to show that our method achieves significant speedups over naive retraining, with minimal impact on utility.

</details>


### [145] [Learning Rate Scaling across LoRA Ranks and Transfer to Full Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06204)
*Nan Chen,Soledad Villar,Soufiane Hayou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了Maximal-Update Adaptation (μA)理论框架，用于确定LoRA微调中学习率随适配器秩的最优缩放规则，并发现可将LoRA调优的学习率可靠地迁移到全参数微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA作为参数高效微调的标准工具，其训练动态复杂且依赖于多个超参数（初始化、适配器秩、学习率等）。特别是最优学习率如何随适配器秩缩放尚不明确，导致每次改变秩时都需要重新调整学习率，增加了调优成本。

Method: 提出Maximal-Update Adaptation (μA)理论框架，灵感来源于预训练中的Maximal-Update Parametrization (μP)。利用超参数迁移技术分析学习率随模型宽度和适配器秩的缩放模式，识别出两种不同机制：学习率基本不变和与秩成反比缩放。

Result: 理论分析揭示了最优学习率随秩的不同缩放模式，并确定了一种配置允许将LoRA调优的学习率迁移到全参数微调。在语言、视觉、视觉-语言、图像生成和强化学习任务上的实验验证了缩放规则的有效性，并证明LoRA调优的学习率可可靠迁移到全参数微调。

Conclusion: μA框架为LoRA微调提供了理论指导，显著降低了学习率调优成本，特别是通过将LoRA调优的学习率迁移到全参数微调，大幅减少了全参数微调的调优开销。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a standard tool for parameter-efficient finetuning of large models. While it induces a small memory footprint, its training dynamics can be surprisingly complex as they depend on several hyperparameters such as initialization, adapter rank, and learning rate. In particular, it is unclear how the optimal learning rate scales with adapter rank, which forces practitioners to re-tune the learning rate whenever the rank is changed. In this paper, we introduce Maximal-Update Adaptation ($μ$A), a theoretical framework that characterizes how the "optimal" learning rate should scale with model width and adapter rank to produce stable, non-vanishing feature updates under standard configurations. $μ$A is inspired from the Maximal-Update Parametrization ($μ$P) in pretraining. Our analysis leverages techniques from hyperparameter transfer and reveals that the optimal learning rate exhibits different scaling patterns depending on initialization and LoRA scaling factor. Specifically, we identify two regimes: one where the optimal learning rate remains roughly invariant across ranks, and another where it scales inversely with rank. We further identify a configuration that allows learning rate transfer from LoRA to full finetuning, drastically reducing the cost of learning rate tuning for full finetuning. Experiments across language, vision, vision--language, image generation, and reinforcement learning tasks validate our scaling rules and show that learning rates tuned on LoRA transfer reliably to full finetuning.

</details>


### [146] [On the Convergence of Multicalibration Gradient Boosting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06773)
*Daniel Haimovich,Fridolin Linder,Lorenzo Perini,Niek Tax,Milan Vojnovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多标定梯度提升在回归问题中的收敛性分析：证明了预测更新幅度以O(1/√T)衰减，多标定误差具有相同收敛率；在弱学习器平滑条件下可达线性收敛；自适应变体可实现局部二次收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标定梯度提升作为一种可扩展方法已在实践中成功应用，但其收敛性质缺乏理论理解。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为该方法提供严格的收敛保证。

Method: 分析多标定梯度提升在平方误差损失回归问题中的收敛性质。通过理论证明预测更新幅度的衰减速率，研究弱学习器平滑性对收敛率的影响，分析自适应变体的收敛行为，并探讨保持收敛性的重新缩放方案。

Result: 证明了多标定梯度提升的预测更新幅度以O(1/√T)速率衰减，多标定误差具有相同收敛率。在弱学习器平滑条件下，收敛率可提升至线性收敛。自适应变体可实现训练损失的局部二次收敛。实验验证了理论结果，明确了方法实现快速收敛和强多标定的适用条件。

Conclusion: 本文为多标定梯度提升提供了首个收敛性理论保证，填补了该领域的重要理论空白。理论结果与实验证据一致，明确了该方法在不同条件下的收敛行为，为其实际应用提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Multicalibration gradient boosting has recently emerged as a scalable method that empirically produces approximately multicalibrated predictors and has been deployed at web scale. Despite this empirical success, its convergence properties are not well understood. In this paper, we bridge the gap by providing convergence guarantees for multicalibration gradient boosting in regression with squared-error loss. We show that the magnitude of successive prediction updates decays at $O(1/\sqrt{T})$, which implies the same convergence rate bound for the multicalibration error over rounds. Under additional smoothness assumptions on the weak learners, this rate improves to linear convergence. We further analyze adaptive variants, showing local quadratic convergence of the training loss, and we study rescaling schemes that preserve convergence. Experiments on real-world datasets support our theory and clarify the regimes in which the method achieves fast convergence and strong multicalibration.

</details>


### [147] [Multi-Way Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06205)
*Akshit Achara,Tatiana Gaintseva,Mateo Mahaut,Pritish Chakraborty,Viktor Stenby Johansson,Melih Barsbey,Emanuele Rodolà,Donato Crisostomi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出GCPA方法解决多模型对齐问题，在保持几何结构的同时优化检索性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型对齐方法存在两个主要问题：1）基于成对映射的方法在模型数量增加时计算复杂度呈二次方增长；2）缺乏一致的全局参考空间。需要一种能同时处理三个及以上模型对齐的方法。

Method: 论文提出三阶段方法：1）首先将广义普氏分析（GPA）扩展到多模型对齐，构建共享正交空间以保持内部几何结构；2）分析发现严格等距对齐对检索任务次优，而CCA等一致性最大化方法更有效；3）提出几何校正普氏对齐（GCPA），先建立稳健的GPA基础空间，再进行后处理校正方向不匹配。

Result: 大量实验表明，GCPA在保持实用共享参考空间的同时，持续改进任意到任意检索性能，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GCPA为多模型对齐提供了有效解决方案，既能构建一致的全局参考空间，又能优化检索任务性能，解决了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis suggests that independently trained neural networks converge to increasingly similar latent spaces. However, current strategies for mapping these representations are inherently pairwise, scaling quadratically with the number of models and failing to yield a consistent global reference. In this paper, we study the alignment of $M \ge 3$ models. We first adapt Generalized Procrustes Analysis (GPA) to construct a shared orthogonal universe that preserves the internal geometry essential for tasks like model stitching. We then show that strict isometric alignment is suboptimal for retrieval, where agreement-maximizing methods like Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA) typically prevail. To bridge this gap, we finally propose Geometry-Corrected Procrustes Alignment (GCPA), which establishes a robust GPA-based universe followed by a post-hoc correction for directional mismatch. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GCPA consistently improves any-to-any retrieval while retaining a practical shared reference space.

</details>


### [148] [Robust Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06775)
*Sajad Ashkezari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了在对抗性扰动输入下的鲁棒分类器学习问题，其中干净数据和标签也是对抗性选择的，将其建模为在线学习问题，定义了新的维度来刻画可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒PAC学习研究通常假设干净数据和标签是良性的，但实际中攻击者可能同时操纵干净数据和扰动。本文旨在研究更一般的对抗性设置，其中数据和标签都是对抗性选择的，这更贴近现实安全威胁场景。

Method: 将问题建模为在线学习框架，考虑可实现和不可知学习性。定义新的维度（类似于Littlestone维度）来刻画假设类的复杂性。将该维度推广到多分类假设类，并研究学习者不知道每个点允许的扰动集合、只有先验信息的情况。

Result: 定义了新的维度，证明其控制可实现设置中的错误界限和不可知设置中的遗憾界限。与PAC学习中的维度不同，新维度更简单且类似于Littlestone维度。将维度推广到多分类假设类，在可实现情况下得到类似结果。

Conclusion: 本文提出了在对抗性数据和扰动下的鲁棒分类器学习新框架，定义了控制学习性能的新维度，为理解这类对抗性在线学习问题提供了理论基础，并扩展到更实际的场景（未知扰动集合）。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning robust classifiers where the classifier will receive a perturbed input. Unlike robust PAC learning studied in prior work, here the clean data and its label are also adversarially chosen. We formulate this setting as an online learning problem and consider both the realizable and agnostic learnability of hypothesis classes. We define a new dimension of classes and show it controls the mistake bounds in the realizable setting and the regret bounds in the agnostic setting. In contrast to the dimension that characterizes learnability in the PAC setting, our dimension is rather simple and resembles the Littlestone dimension. We generalize our dimension to multiclass hypothesis classes and prove similar results in the realizable case. Finally, we study the case where the learner does not know the set of allowed perturbations for each point and only has some prior on them.

</details>


### [149] [Learning Deep Hybrid Models with Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06837)
*Naoya Takeishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于锐度感知最小化(SAM)的混合建模方法，通过关注损失最小值的平坦性来避免科学模型被机器学习模型忽略的问题，实现更简单的模型结构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合建模结合机器学习模型和科学数学模型，但机器学习模型的灵活性可能导致科学模型在预测中被忽略，使混合建模失去意义。现有正则化方法严重依赖模型架构和领域知识。

Method: 将锐度感知最小化(SAM)思想应用于混合建模设置，关注损失最小值的平坦性，使模型尽可能简单，避免科学模型被机器学习模型主导。

Result: 数值实验表明，基于SAM的方法在不同模型选择和数据集上表现良好，能够有效平衡机器学习模型和科学模型的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过关注损失最小值的平坦性，SAM方法为混合建模提供了一种通用且有效的正则化策略，不依赖特定模型架构或领域知识。

Abstract: Hybrid modeling, the combination of machine learning models and scientific mathematical models, enables flexible and robust data-driven prediction with partial interpretability. However, effectively the scientific models may be ignored in prediction due to the flexibility of the machine learning model, making the idea of hybrid modeling pointless. Typically some regularization is applied to hybrid model learning to avoid such a failure case, but the formulation of the regularizer strongly depends on model architectures and domain knowledge. In this paper, we propose to focus on the flatness of loss minima in learning hybrid models, aiming to make the model as simple as possible. We employ the idea of sharpness-aware minimization and adapt it to the hybrid modeling setting. Numerical experiments show that the SAM-based method works well across different choices of models and datasets.

</details>


### [150] [SR4-Fit: An Interpretable and Informative Classification Algorithm Applied to Prediction of U.S. House of Representatives Elections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06229)
*Shyam Sundar Murali Krishnan,Dean Frederick Hougen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SR4-Fit是一种新型可解释分类算法，通过稀疏松弛正则化回归规则拟合，在保持优异分类性能的同时解决了传统规则算法预测能力不足和不稳定的问题，在选举预测等多个数据集上超越了黑盒模型和现有可解释算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在关键应用中需要可解释模型，但高性能模型通常是"黑盒"系统，而传统规则算法如RuleFit虽然简单但预测能力不足且不稳定。需要开发既能保持优异预测性能又具有可解释性的算法。

Method: 提出SR4-Fit（稀疏松弛正则化回归规则拟合）算法，这是一种新型可解释分类算法。使用美国国会选区的人口特征数据（来自美国人口普查局美国社区调查）来预测众议院选举党派结果，并在六个公开分类数据集（乳腺癌、大肠杆菌、页面块、皮马印第安人、车辆、酵母）上进一步验证。

Result: SR4-Fit在预测众议院选举党派结果方面达到了前所未有的准确性和可解释性。虽然多数党仍然是最强预测因子，但SR4-Fit揭示了影响预测结果的人口因素内在组合，这些在黑盒算法（如随机森林）中无法解释。SR4-Fit在准确性、简洁性和鲁棒性方面均超越了黑盒模型和现有可解释规则算法（如RuleFit），生成稳定且可解释的规则集，同时保持优异的预测性能。

Conclusion: SR4-Fit算法解决了模型可解释性与预测能力之间的传统权衡问题，为关键应用提供了既高性能又可解释的机器学习解决方案，在选举预测等多个领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: The growth of machine learning demands interpretable models for critical applications, yet most high-performing models are ``black-box'' systems that obscure input-output relationships, while traditional rule-based algorithms like RuleFit suffer from a lack of predictive power and instability despite their simplicity. This motivated our development of Sparse Relaxed Regularized Regression Rule-Fit (SR4-Fit), a novel interpretable classification algorithm that addresses these limitations while maintaining superior classification performance. Using demographic characteristics of U.S. congressional districts from the Census Bureau's American Community Survey, we demonstrate that SR4-Fit can predict House election party outcomes with unprecedented accuracy and interpretability. Our results show that while the majority party remains the strongest predictor, SR4-Fit has revealed intrinsic combinations of demographic factors that affect prediction outcomes that were unable to be interpreted in black-box algorithms such as random forests. The SR4-Fit algorithm surpasses both black-box models and existing interpretable rule-based algorithms such as RuleFit with respect to accuracy, simplicity, and robustness, generating stable and interpretable rule sets while maintaining superior predictive performance, thus addressing the traditional trade-off between model interpretability and predictive capability in electoral forecasting. To further validate SR4-Fit's performance, we also apply it to six additional publicly available classification datasets, like the breast cancer, Ecoli, page blocks, Pima Indians, vehicle, and yeast datasets, and find similar results.

</details>


### [151] [Vision Transformer Finetuning Benefits from Non-Smooth Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06883)
*Ambroise Odonnat,Laetitia Chapel,Romain Tavenard,Ievgen Redko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了视觉Transformer组件的可塑性（对输入变化的适应能力），发现高可塑性（低平滑性）的注意力模块和前馈层在微调中表现更好，这与传统认为平滑性有益的观点不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Transformer架构的平滑性在泛化、训练稳定性和对抗鲁棒性方面已被广泛研究，但其在迁移学习中的作用仍不清楚。本文旨在分析视觉Transformer组件适应输入变化的能力（即可塑性），为迁移学习中的组件选择提供理论指导。

Method: 将可塑性定义为平均变化率，捕捉对输入扰动的敏感性（高可塑性意味着低平滑性）。通过理论分析和综合实验，研究不同Transformer组件的可塑性特性，特别是注意力模块和前馈层的表现。

Result: 研究发现注意力模块和前馈层具有高可塑性，这种特性在微调过程中能带来更好的性能表现。这为实践中选择优先适应的组件提供了原则性指导。

Conclusion: 研究挑战了平滑性总是有益的普遍假设，提出了从可塑性角度理解Transformer功能特性的新视角。高可塑性的组件在迁移学习中更值得优先适应，这对实践者有重要指导意义。

Abstract: The smoothness of the transformer architecture has been extensively studied in the context of generalization, training stability, and adversarial robustness. However, its role in transfer learning remains poorly understood. In this paper, we analyze the ability of vision transformer components to adapt their outputs to changes in inputs, or, in other words, their plasticity. Defined as an average rate of change, it captures the sensitivity to input perturbation; in particular, a high plasticity implies low smoothness. We demonstrate through theoretical analysis and comprehensive experiments that this perspective provides principled guidance in choosing the components to prioritize during adaptation. A key takeaway for practitioners is that the high plasticity of the attention modules and feedforward layers consistently leads to better finetuning performance. Our findings depart from the prevailing assumption that smoothness is desirable, offering a novel perspective on the functional properties of transformers. The code is available at https://github.com/ambroiseodt/vit-plasticity.

</details>


### [152] [RuleSmith: Multi-Agent LLMs for Automated Game Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06232)
*Ziyao Zeng,Chen Liu,Tianyu Liu,Hao Wang,Xiatao Sun,Fengyu Yang,Xiaofeng Liu,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RuleSmith：首个利用多智能体LLM推理能力实现自动化游戏平衡的框架，通过游戏引擎、LLM自博弈和贝叶斯优化在规则空间中进行参数调优


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统游戏平衡需要大量人工测试、专家直觉和手动调整，过程耗时且低效。需要自动化方法来替代这一繁琐过程，特别是在包含异质派系、经济系统、生产规则和战斗机制的复杂多智能体环境中

Method: 结合游戏引擎、多智能体LLM自博弈和贝叶斯优化。LLM智能体通过解析文本规则书和游戏状态生成行动，快速评估胜率差异等平衡指标。采用基于获取函数的自适应采样和离散投影的贝叶斯优化：有希望的候选配置获得更多评估游戏以准确评估，探索性候选获得较少游戏以提高探索效率

Result: RuleSmith在CivMini（简化的文明风格游戏）上验证，能够收敛到高度平衡的配置，并提供可直接应用于下游游戏系统的可解释规则调整。实验表明框架有效实现了自动化平衡

Conclusion: LLM模拟可以作为复杂多智能体环境中自动化设计和平衡的强大替代方案。RuleSmith框架展示了利用多智能体LLM推理能力实现游戏平衡自动化的可行性，为游戏设计提供了新的自动化工具

Abstract: Game balancing is a longstanding challenge requiring repeated playtesting, expert intuition, and extensive manual tuning. We introduce RuleSmith, the first framework that achieves automated game balancing by leveraging the reasoning capabilities of multi-agent LLMs. It couples a game engine, multi-agent LLMs self-play, and Bayesian optimization operating over a multi-dimensional rule space. As a proof of concept, we instantiate RuleSmith on CivMini, a simplified civilization-style game containing heterogeneous factions, economy systems, production rules, and combat mechanics, all governed by tunable parameters. LLM agents interpret textual rulebooks and game states to generate actions, to conduct fast evaluation of balance metrics such as win-rate disparities. To search the parameter landscape efficiently, we integrate Bayesian optimization with acquisition-based adaptive sampling and discrete projection: promising candidates receive more evaluation games for accurate assessment, while exploratory candidates receive fewer games for efficient exploration. Experiments show that RuleSmith converges to highly balanced configurations and provides interpretable rule adjustments that can be directly applied to downstream game systems. Our results illustrate that LLM simulation can serve as a powerful surrogate for automating design and balancing in complex multi-agent environments.

</details>


### [153] [Sample Complexity of Causal Identification with Temporal Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06899)
*Ameya Rathod,Sujay Belsare,Salvik Krishna Nautiyal,Dhruv Laad,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将时间序列动态和多环境异质性结合作为因果图识别的互补信息源，建立了统一的必要可识别性条件，并分析了薄尾与厚尾噪声下的统计恢复极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据中恢复唯一因果图是一个不适定问题，因为多个生成机制可能导致相同的观测分布。现有研究分别利用时间序列动态或多环境异质性来约束这一问题，但缺乏对两者整合的系统分析。

Method: 整合时间序列动态和多环境异质性作为互补的异质性来源，建立统一的必要可识别性条件，分析薄尾（高斯）和厚尾（Student's t分布）噪声下的统计恢复极限，推导信息论边界。

Result: 时间结构可以替代缺失的环境多样性，即使在异质性不足的情况下也可能实现可识别性。对于厚尾分布，几何可识别性条件保持不变，但样本复杂度与高斯基线显著偏离，存在稳健性代价。

Conclusion: 该研究将重点从因果结构是否可识别转向其在实际中是否可统计恢复，为现实非平稳系统中的因果图恢复方法建立了基本极限，特别关注基于协方差的方法在厚尾噪声下的性能。

Abstract: Recovering a unique causal graph from observational data is an ill-posed problem because multiple generating mechanisms can lead to the same observational distribution. This problem becomes solvable only by exploiting specific structural or distributional assumptions. While recent work has separately utilized time-series dynamics or multi-environment heterogeneity to constrain this problem, we integrate both as complementary sources of heterogeneity. This integration yields unified necessary identifiability conditions and enables a rigorous analysis of the statistical limits of recovery under thin versus heavy-tailed noise. In particular, temporal structure is shown to effectively substitute for missing environmental diversity, possibly achieving identifiability even under insufficient heterogeneity. Extending this analysis to heavy-tailed (Student's t) distributions, we demonstrate that while geometric identifiability conditions remain invariant, the sample complexity diverges significantly from the Gaussian baseline.
